- **council-shankara**: 議會成員·商羯羅(吠檀多·不二一元論 Advaita)。當議會要呈現印度教吠檀多內部分歧、或需要梵我不二/世界為幻的不二論立場時使用。通常由 council-moderator 調度。 ([F-e-u-e-r/worldwide-council](https://github.com/F-e-u-e-r/worldwide-council/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/council-shankara.md) · ★1)

- **council-ship-maintain**: Council task posture: shipping and maintainability. Method: compare viable options by delivery effort, practical failure modes, and maintenance ownership over a year; include the cost of doing nothing. Judge whether extra complexity buys a needed capability rather than assuming smaller is better. Blind spot: overlooking system-level effects of a locally workable choice; name one such consequence. End with an explicit position, its evidence, and what would change it. No option is preferred in advance; agreement is as valid as disagreement. ([vantt/forgent](https://github.com/vantt/forgent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/council-ship-maintain.md))

- **council-skeptic**: Council member that argues from the failure mode — what breaks, who notices, and how it is found. Dispatched by the council skill with a topic brief. - **council-skeptic**: The skeptical advisor's chair of the Deal Council, the Kevin Tuttle seat. Ask for it before any recommendation reaches a client, and especially when the packet looks finished: "would this survive the client's advisor," "run the skeptic chair," "poke holes in this recommendation," "what will the CPA ask," "the client went quiet," "we pay you guys to do this," "pressure-test the deal packet." It asks the hard money questions the client's own advisor asks, including the ones pointed at CARR's fee. It needs no data to work, which is the point. Dispatched by the orchestrator only, with "Run dir: &lt;path&gt;. Lot: &lt;id&gt;. Follow-up: &lt;yes|no&gt;."; not for direct use. - **cqa-browser**: Sub-agent of /gm:checklist-quality-audit. Dispatched by the orchestrator only, with "Run dir: &lt;path&gt;. Lot: &lt;id&gt;. Follow-up: &lt;yes|no&gt;."; not for direct use. - **cqa-fix-reviewer**: Sub-agent of /gm:checklist-quality-audit fix. Dispatched by the orchestrator only, with "Run dir: &lt;path&gt;. Review: &lt;repo&gt;. Worktree: &lt;path&gt;. Base: &lt;sha&gt;. Pass: &lt;N&gt;."; not for direct use. ([gingerminds/claude-skills](https://github.com/gingerminds/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/cqa-fix-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **cqa-fixer**: Sub-agent of /gm:checklist-quality-audit fix. Corrects the rows of one section (one checklist category) of a repo, the key, in the repo's git worktree, one guarded commit per row carrying the section and the row's ref, then writes fix/&lt;repo&gt;/report-&lt;N&gt;.json in the run directory and validates it. Dispatched by the orchestrator only, with "Run dir: &lt;path&gt;. Fix: &lt;repo&gt;. Section: &lt;alias&gt;. Worktree: &lt;path&gt;. Pass: &lt;N&gt;. Rows: CQ-3, CQ-12. Notes: &lt;path|none&gt;. Stack core: &lt;path|none&gt;."; not for direct use. ([gingerminds/claude-skills](https://github.com/gingerminds/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/cqa-fixer.md) · ★2)
- **cqa-importer**: Sub-agent of /gm:checklist-quality-audit. Reads one context document (a third-party audit report: an RGAA grid, a pentest report...) as untrusted data and writes, in the run directory, either its layout (context-docs/&lt;id&gt;/layout.json) or the points of one sheet (context-docs/&lt;id&gt;/parts/&lt;NN&gt;.json). Dispatched by the orchestrator only, with "Run dir: &lt;path&gt;. Document: &lt;id&gt;. Step: &lt;layout|part&gt;. Sheet: &lt;"nom"|none&gt;."; not for direct use.
